  <script type="importmap">
  {
    "imports": {
      "three": "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.185.0/build/three.module.js",
      "three/addons/": "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.185.0/examples/jsm/"

  <script>
  // Mobile HTML viewers sometimes expose a partial console object where
  // console.warn/error/info exist but are not callable. Normalize them before
  // any startup diagnostics can accidentally crash the game.
  (() => {
    const root = window;
    const c = (root.console && typeof root.console === 'object') ? root.console : {};
    const noop = function() {};
    for (const name of ['log','info','warn','error','debug','trace','group','groupEnd']) {
      if (typeof c[name] !== 'function') c[name] = noop;
    }
    root.console = c;

    // Small DOM compatibility shims for restrictive in-app HTML viewers.
    if (typeof Element !== 'undefined') {
      if (typeof Element.prototype.remove !== 'function') {
        Element.prototype.remove = function() {
          if (this.parentNode) this.parentNode.removeChild(this);
        };
      }
      if (typeof Element.prototype.closest !== 'function') {
        Element.prototype.closest = function(selector) {
          let node = this;
          while (node && node.nodeType === 1) {
            const matches = node.matches || node.msMatchesSelector || node.webkitMatchesSelector;
            if (matches && matches.call(node, selector)) return node;
            node = node.parentElement;
          }
          return null;
        };
      }
    }
    if (typeof root.requestAnimationFrame !== 'function') {
      root.requestAnimationFrame = callback => setTimeout(() => callback(performance.now()), 16);
      root.cancelAnimationFrame = id => clearTimeout(id);
    }

    root.safeConsoleCall = function(method) {
      try {
        const args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
        const fn = root.console && root.console[method];
        if (typeof fn === 'function') fn.apply(root.console, args);
      } catch (_) {}
    };
    root.safeConsoleInfo = function() { root.safeConsoleCall.apply(null, ['info'].concat(Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments))); };
    root.safeConsoleWarn = function() { root.safeConsoleCall.apply(null, ['warn'].concat(Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments))); };
    root.safeConsoleError = function() { root.safeConsoleCall.apply(null, ['error'].concat(Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments))); };
  })();
  // Explicit bindings for embedded viewers whose global-property lookup is incomplete.
  var safeConsoleInfo = window.safeConsoleInfo;
  var safeConsoleWarn = window.safeConsoleWarn;
  var safeConsoleError = window.safeConsoleError;
  </script>
  <div id="loadingScreen" role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-busy="true">
    <div class="loadingCard">
      <div class="loadingLogo">CAT<span>STRIKE</span></div>
      <div id="loadingStatus">STARTING GAME...</div>
      <div id="loadingDetail">Preparing the game shell.</div>

  <script>
  (() => {
    const screen = document.getElementById('loadingScreen');
    const fill = document.getElementById('loadingFill');
    const status = document.getElementById('loadingStatus');
    const detail = document.getElementById('loadingDetail');
    const percent = document.getElementById('loadingPercent');
    let finished = false;
    const set = (value, text, extra='') => {
      if (finished) return;
      const p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(Number(value) || 0)));
      fill.style.transform = `scaleX(${p/100})`;
      percent.textContent = `${p}%`;
      if (text) status.textContent = text;
      if (extra) detail.textContent = extra;
    };
    const fail = error => {
      if (finished) return;
      screen.classList.add('failed');
      set(100, 'LOADING FAILED', String(error?.message || error || 'Unknown startup error'));
      const play = document.getElementById('playBtn');
      const deploy = document.getElementById('deployBtn');
      if (play) { play.disabled = true; play.querySelector('span') ? play.querySelector('span').textContent = 'STARTUP ERROR' : play.textContent = 'STARTUP ERROR'; }
      if (deploy) { deploy.disabled = true; deploy.textContent = 'STARTUP ERROR'; }
    };
    const ready = () => {
      if (finished) return;
      set(100, 'READY', 'Rig, textures, controls, audio buffers, and shaders are prepared.');
      finished = true;
      screen.setAttribute('aria-busy','false');
      setTimeout(() => screen.classList.add('done'), 180);
      setTimeout(() => { if (screen.parentNode) screen.remove(); }, 900);
    };
    window.CatStrikeLoading = { set, fail, ready, get finished(){ return finished; } };
    addEventListener('error', event => { if (!finished) fail(event.error || event.message); }, true);
    addEventListener('unhandledrejection', event => { if (!finished) fail(event.reason); });
    set(2, 'STARTING GAME...', 'Loading the embedded game code.');
  })();
  </script>

        <div class="settingRow"><label for="mouseAdsSensitivity">Mouse ADS multiplier<small>Applied while scoped / aiming</small></label><input id="mouseAdsSensitivity" type="range" min="0.20" max="1.20" step="0.01"><output id="mouseAdsSensitivityValue">0.61×</output></div>
        <div class="settingRow"><label for="touchSensitivity">Touch sensitivity<small>Drag-to-look speed on mobile</small></label><input id="touchSensitivity" type="range" min="0.20" max="3.00" step="0.01"><output id="touchSensitivityValue">1.00×</output></div>
        <div class="settingRow"><label for="touchAdsSensitivity">Touch ADS multiplier<small>Scoped mobile precision</small></label><input id="touchAdsSensitivity" type="range" min="0.20" max="1.20" step="0.01"><output id="touchAdsSensitivityValue">0.59×</output></div>
        <div class="settingRow settingToggle"><label for="invertLookY">Invert vertical look<small>Applies to mouse and touch</small></label><input id="invertLookY" type="checkbox"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="settingsFooter"><button id="settingsReset" type="button">RESET INPUT DEFAULTS</button></div>
    </section>
  </div>

  <div id="result">
    <div class="card"><div class="hero">
      <h1 id="resultTitle">MATCH <span>VICTORY</span></h1>
      <p class="subtitle" id="resultText"></p>
      <div class="resultActions"><button id="restartBtn">PLAY AGAIN</button><button id="changeModeBtn" class="secondaryButton" type="button">CHANGE MODE</button></div>
    </div></div>
  </div>

  <script type="module">
    import * as THREE from 'three';
    import { mergeGeometries } from 'three/addons/utils/BufferGeometryUtils.js';

    const safeConsoleCall = (method, ...args) => {
      try {
        const fn = window.console && window.console[method];
        if (typeof fn === 'function') fn.apply(window.console, args);
      } catch (_) {}
    };
    const safeConsoleInfo = (...args) => safeConsoleCall('info', ...args);
    const safeConsoleWarn = (...args) => safeConsoleCall('warn', ...args);
    const safeConsoleError = (...args) => safeConsoleCall('error', ...args);

    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const gameRoot = $('game');
    const menu = $('menu');
    const pauseScreen = $('pause');
    const resultScreen = $('result');
    const settingsMenu = $('settingsMenu');
    // Input capability and rendering budget are separate concerns.
    // A narrow desktop preview still has a mouse and must retain Pointer Lock.
    const primaryFinePointer = matchMedia('(pointer: fine)').matches;
    const primaryCoarsePointer = matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
    const coarsePointer = primaryCoarsePointer && !primaryFinePointer;
    // Hybrid Windows devices can report the touchscreen as the primary pointer
    // even while a real mouse is in use. The trusted mouse pointerdown happens
    // before Play/Deploy click handlers, so desktop controls activate immediately.
    let physicalMouseDetected = primaryFinePointer;
    const usingMobileControls = () => coarsePointer && !physicalMouseDetected;
    addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
      if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') physicalMouseDetected = true;
    }, {capture:true, passive:true});
    const lowSpec = coarsePointer || innerWidth < 820 ||
      (navigator.deviceMemory && navigator.deviceMemory <= 4) ||
      (navigator.hardwareConcurrency && navigator.hardwareConcurrency <= 4);
    const loadingUI = window.CatStrikeLoading;
    loadingUI?.set(8, 'STARTING ENGINE...', lowSpec ? 'Mobile/low-power quality profile selected.' : 'Desktop quality profile selected.');
    await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));

    // ---------- Character model startup ----------
    // R111: the current CatStrike character path intentionally uses only the
    // no-legs static Boykisser geometry. createBot() hardcodes rigged = null and
    // no live call site constructs the former skinned GLB rig. R110 nevertheless
    // embedded ~794k characters of dead GLB Base64, imported GLTFLoader and
    // SkeletonUtils, decoded/parsed the GLB, normalized its materials, computed
    // bounds, and built animation clips before the renderer could start. Remove
    // that unreachable startup payload/path while preserving the successful-path
    // loading UI messages and the actual character/gameplay model unchanged.
    loadingUI?.set(16, 'UNPACKING THE CAT WIG...', 'Decoding the embedded skinned Boykisser model.');
    loadingUI?.set(42, 'MODEL READY', 'Skeleton, skin, and animations loaded.');

    // Cap real GPU pixels and allow the adaptive scaler to go below native CSS
    // resolution when the measured frame rate is poor. The previous Math.max(1)
    // clamp made the performance governor ineffective on the exact laptops that
    // needed it most. CSS still presents the same full-size canvas.
    const MAX_RENDER_PIXELS = lowSpec ? 1050000 : 2600000;
    const MIN_DEVICE_PIXEL_RATIO = lowSpec ? .64 : .72;
    function getSharpBasePixelRatio() {
      const displayDpr = Math.max(1, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
      const cssPixels = Math.max(1, innerWidth * innerHeight);
      const budgetDpr = Math.sqrt(MAX_RENDER_PIXELS / cssPixels);
      return Math.min(displayDpr, lowSpec ? 1.08 : 1.32, Math.max(1, budgetDpr));
    }
    const MIN_RENDER_SCALE = lowSpec ? .60 : .70;
    let renderScale = lowSpec ? .82 : 1;
    let appliedRenderPixelRatio = -1;

    // ---------- Renderer / scene ----------
    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
      antialias: !coarsePointer && !lowSpec,
      powerPreference: 'high-performance',
      precision: lowSpec ? 'mediump' : 'highp',
      stencil: false
    });
    renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
    function applyRenderScale() {
      const ratio = Math.max(MIN_DEVICE_PIXEL_RATIO, getSharpBasePixelRatio() * renderScale);
      // setPixelRatio() internally resizes the drawing buffer. Avoid repeating that
      // allocation when a caller re-applies an already-active performance level.
      if(Math.abs(ratio-appliedRenderPixelRatio)>.0001){renderer.setPixelRatio(ratio);appliedRenderPixelRatio=ratio;}
      renderer.domElement.style.imageRendering = 'auto';
    }
    applyRenderScale();
    // Static shadow atlas + filmic tone mapping. The shadow map is rendered
    // once because moving characters no longer cast expensive dynamic shadows.
    renderer.shadowMap.enabled = !lowSpec;
    renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFShadowMap;
    // Every remaining shadow caster is static. Render the shadow atlas once
    // instead of re-rendering the whole map into it on every frame.
    renderer.shadowMap.autoUpdate = false;
    renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true;
    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.08;
    renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    renderer.domElement.id = 'world';
    renderer.domElement.tabIndex = 0;
    gameRoot.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
    loadingUI?.set(55, 'RENDERER READY', 'GPU canvas and sharp mobile-safe resolution are initialized.');
    await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));

    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x8fb4c6);
    scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(0xd7c5a4, 58, 182);

    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(72, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.05, 320);
    camera.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
    camera.layers.enable(2); // exact skinned player meshes
    camera.layers.enable(3); // name tags / team rings / lightweight decoration
    camera.layers.enable(4); // moving weapons/objectives that must occlude outlines
    scene.add(camera);

    // ---------- True skinned-model silhouette outline ----------
    // User preference: remove the added black character outlines entirely. Keep
    // the codepath available for future edits, but disable the rendered outline
    // pass so the game shows only the actual model surface.
    const ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES = false;
    // Render actual animated character triangles to a low-resolution mask, after
    // first populating the same target's depth buffer with world geometry. A
    // fullscreen edge shader then draws only the pixels just OUTSIDE that mask.
    // This follows ears, limbs, tail, crouches, running poses, and every bone,
    // while never expanding black geometry around the camera.
    const MODEL_OUTLINE_LAYER = 2;
    const DECORATION_LAYER = 3;
    const MODEL_OUTLINE_DYNAMIC_OCCLUDER_LAYER = 4;
    // The feature remains enabled, but its mask is intentionally cheaper than the
    // main frame and adapts further only when measured FPS proves it necessary.
    let modelOutlineScale = lowSpec ? .44 : .64;
    let modelOutlineHz = lowSpec ? 20 : 36;
    const modelOutlineTarget = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(2, 2, {
      depthBuffer: true,
      stencilBuffer: false,
      minFilter: THREE.NearestFilter,
      magFilter: THREE.NearestFilter,
      generateMipmaps: false
    });
    modelOutlineTarget.texture.name = 'CatStrike.ExactPlayerSilhouetteMask';
    modelOutlineTarget.texture.colorSpace = THREE.NoColorSpace;

    const modelOutlineDepthMaterial = new THREE.MeshDepthMaterial({
      depthPacking: THREE.BasicDepthPacking,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide,
      depthTest: true,
      depthWrite: true
    });
    modelOutlineDepthMaterial.colorWrite = false;
    modelOutlineDepthMaterial.blending = THREE.NoBlending;

    const modelOutlineMaskMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: 0xffffff,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide,
      depthTest: true,
      depthWrite: false,
      fog: false,
      toneMapped: false,
      blending: THREE.NoBlending
    });

    const exactOutlineOccluderScene = new THREE.Scene();
    const exactOutlineDynamicOccluderScene = new THREE.Scene();
    let exactOutlineOccluderMesh = null;
    let exactOutlineBannerOccluder = null;
    let exactOutlineFountainOccluder = null;
    let exactOutlineDynamicDirty = true;
    const modelOutlineCompositeScene = new THREE.Scene();
    const modelOutlineCompositeCamera = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-1, 1, 1, -1, 0, 1);
    const modelOutlineCompositeMaterial = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: {
        maskTexture: { value: modelOutlineTarget.texture },
        texelSize: { value: new THREE.Vector2(.5, .5) },
        edgeOpacity: { value: .94 }
      },
      vertexShader: `
        varying vec2 vUv;
        void main() {
          vUv = uv;
          gl_Position = vec4(position.xy, 0.0, 1.0);
        }
      `,
      fragmentShader: `
        precision mediump float;
        uniform sampler2D maskTexture;
        uniform vec2 texelSize;
        uniform float edgeOpacity;
        varying vec2 vUv;

        float maskAt(vec2 uv) { return step(0.5, texture2D(maskTexture, uv).r); }

        void main() {
          float center = maskAt(vUv);
          float around = 0.0;
          around = max(around, maskAt(vUv + texelSize * vec2(-1.0,  0.0)));
          around = max(around, maskAt(vUv + texelSize * vec2( 1.0,  0.0)));
          around = max(around, maskAt(vUv + texelSize * vec2( 0.0, -1.0)));
          around = max(around, maskAt(vUv + texelSize * vec2( 0.0,  1.0)));
          // One exact mask pixel outside the visible animated silhouette. Nearest
          // sampling prevents hidden-player color from bleeding across a wall.
          float outerEdge = around * (1.0 - center);
          if (outerEdge < 0.5) discard;
          gl_FragColor = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, edgeOpacity);
        }
      `,
      transparent: true,
      depthTest: false,
      depthWrite: false,
      toneMapped: false,
      fog: false,
      blending: THREE.NormalBlending
    });
    const modelOutlineQuad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2), modelOutlineCompositeMaterial);
    modelOutlineQuad.frustumCulled = false;
    modelOutlineQuad.updateMatrix();modelOutlineQuad.matrixAutoUpdate=false;modelOutlineQuad.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;
    modelOutlineCompositeScene.add(modelOutlineQuad);

    let nextExactOutlineMaskAt = 0;
    let exactOutlineMaskReady = false;
    const exactOutlineSavedClear = new THREE.Color();
    const exactOutlineCameraWorld = new THREE.Vector3();
    const exactOutlineFrustumMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
    const exactOutlineFrustum = new THREE.Frustum();
    let exactOutlineCameraFrameAt = -1;
    function refreshExactOutlineCameraFrame(nowSeconds){
      if(nowSeconds===exactOutlineCameraFrameAt)return;
      exactOutlineCameraFrameAt=nowSeconds;
      camera.updateMatrixWorld();
      exactOutlineCameraWorld.setFromMatrixPosition(camera.matrixWorld);
      exactOutlineFrustumMatrix.multiplyMatrices(camera.projectionMatrix,camera.matrixWorldInverse);
      exactOutlineFrustum.setFromProjectionMatrix(exactOutlineFrustumMatrix);
    }
    function botIntersectsCameraFrustum(bot,nowSeconds){
      // Rig LOD, nametag visibility and the outline pass can all ask this exact
      // question during the same animation timestamp. Cache the immutable result
      // for that bot/frame so one sphere/frustum test serves all three systems.
      // R119: the bot culling sphere has a permanent radius (2.35) and Y center
      // (1.12). THREE.Frustum.intersectsSphere() only walks the six planes and
      // evaluates Plane.distanceToPoint(center) < -radius. Evaluate that identical
      // scalar expression directly from bot X/Z so this hot path no longer mutates
      // a shared Sphere/Vector3 or enters two generic helper methods per plane. Plane
      // order, arithmetic order, strict comparison, cache timing and culling result
      // are unchanged.
      if(bot._frustumCheckAt===nowSeconds)return bot._frustumVisibleCached;
      refreshExactOutlineCameraFrame(nowSeconds);
      const x=bot.pos.x,z=bot.pos.z,planes=exactOutlineFrustum.planes;
      let visible=true;
      for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
        const plane=planes[i],normal=plane.normal;
        if(normal.x*x+normal.y*1.12+normal.z*z+plane.constant < -2.35){visible=false;break;}
      }
      bot._frustumCheckAt=nowSeconds;bot._frustumVisibleCached=visible;
      return visible;
    }
    const exactOutlineSampleOffsets = new Float32Array([
       0, 1.72, 0,   0, 1.30, 0,   0, .72, 0,
       0,  .22, 0, -.32, 1.35, 0, .32, 1.35, 0
    ]);
    const exactOutlineVisibilityCache = [];

    function playerCanSeeBotForOutline(bot, nowSeconds = performance.now() * .001, knownFrustumVisible) {
      // R91: updateBots and the outline-mask gate can ask this exact question for
      // the same bot at the same animation timestamp. No caller mutates bot/root/
      // camera visibility state between those two calls, so retain the complete
      // first result for that timestamp and skip the duplicate live/frustum/cache
      // bookkeeping. This is distinct from the lower-frequency LOS cache below:
      // ray cadence and every first query in a frame remain exactly unchanged.
      if (bot._playerVisibilityFrameAt === nowSeconds) return bot._playerVisibilityFrameCached;
      // Corpse roots can now remain visible while dead so the body can settle in world space.
      // Outline/name-tag eligibility must therefore key directly from bot.dead rather than
      // root.visible. Both internal callers iterate configuredActiveBots, whose membership
      // is rebuilt immediately when bot.active changes, so inactive reserve branches remain unreachable.
      if (bot.dead) {
        bot._playerVisibilityFrameAt=nowSeconds;bot._playerVisibilityFrameCached=false;return false;
      }
      // A fully offscreen character cannot contribute an outline or enemy tag.
      // R90: updateBots already performs this exact bot/frame frustum test for rig LOD
      // immediately before enemy-tag visibility. Reuse that exact boolean when supplied
      // instead of re-entering the cached helper; outline-only callers keep the original path.
      // No camera, bot position, or frustum state can change between those two points.
      if(!(knownFrustumVisible===undefined?botIntersectsCameraFrustum(bot,nowSeconds):knownFrustumVisible)){
        bot._playerVisibilityFrameAt=nowSeconds;bot._playerVisibilityFrameCached=false;return false;
      }
      if (bot._playerVisibilityCheckAt && nowSeconds < bot._playerVisibilityCheckAt) {
        const cached=Boolean(bot._playerVisibilityCached);
        bot._playerVisibilityFrameAt=nowSeconds;bot._playerVisibilityFrameCached=cached;return cached;
      }
      bot._playerVisibilityCheckAt = nowSeconds + (lowSpec ? .045 : .028);
      const x = bot.pos.x, z = bot.pos.z;
      // Multiple actual body samples avoid false negatives when only a head, arm,
      // or foot is exposed around cover. Reused numeric storage avoids per-frame
      // arrays and the garbage-collection spikes they caused during 10v10 play.
      const cameraX=exactOutlineCameraWorld.x,cameraY=exactOutlineCameraWorld.y,cameraZ=exactOutlineCameraWorld.z;
      for (let i = 0; i < exactOutlineSampleOffsets.length; i += 3) {
        if (clearVisionRayXYZ(
          cameraX,cameraY,cameraZ,
          x + exactOutlineSampleOffsets[i],
          exactOutlineSampleOffsets[i + 1],
          z + exactOutlineSampleOffsets[i + 2]
        )) {
          bot._playerVisibilityCached = true;
          bot._playerVisibilityFrameAt=nowSeconds;bot._playerVisibilityFrameCached=true;
          return true;
        }
      }
      bot._playerVisibilityCached = false;
      bot._playerVisibilityFrameAt=nowSeconds;bot._playerVisibilityFrameCached=false;
      return false;
    }

    function applyExactOutlineVisibilityGate(nowSeconds) {
      exactOutlineVisibilityCache.length = 0;
      let visibleCount = 0;
      // Inactive reserve bots always have root.visible=false and therefore cannot
      // contribute to the outline render target. configuredActiveBots preserves
      // the original bots[] encounter order, so skip those guaranteed-hidden roots
      // without changing visibility tests or draw ordering for any renderable bot.
      for (const bot of configuredActiveBots) {
        if (!bot?.cat) continue;
        const canSee = playerCanSeeBotForOutline(bot, nowSeconds);
        const animatedVisual = bot.cat;
        exactOutlineVisibilityCache.push(animatedVisual, animatedVisual.visible);
        animatedVisual.visible = animatedVisual.visible && canSee;
        if (animatedVisual.visible) visibleCount++;
      }
      return visibleCount;
    }

    function restoreExactOutlineVisibilityGate() {
      for (let i = 0; i < exactOutlineVisibilityCache.length; i += 2) {
        exactOutlineVisibilityCache[i].visible = exactOutlineVisibilityCache[i + 1];
      }
      exactOutlineVisibilityCache.length = 0;
    }

    function resizeExactModelOutline() {
      const width = Math.max(2, Math.round(innerWidth * modelOutlineScale));
      const height = Math.max(2, Math.round(innerHeight * modelOutlineScale));
      modelOutlineTarget.setSize(width, height);
      modelOutlineCompositeMaterial.uniforms.texelSize.value.set(1 / width, 1 / height);
      exactOutlineMaskReady = false;
      nextExactOutlineMaskAt = 0;
    }

    function updateExactModelOutlineMask(nowSeconds) {
      const interval = 1 / modelOutlineHz;
      if (nowSeconds < nextExactOutlineMaskAt) return;
      nextExactOutlineMaskAt = nowSeconds + interval;

      // Do not run any offscreen depth or composite passes when no outlined
      // character is actually visible to the player. The visibility gate is
      // collider-based and already part of the exact-outline correctness path.
      if (applyExactOutlineVisibilityGate(nowSeconds) === 0) {
        exactOutlineMaskReady = false;
        restoreExactOutlineVisibilityGate();
        return;
      }

      const previousTarget = renderer.getRenderTarget();
      const previousOverride = scene.overrideMaterial;
      const previousLayerMask = camera.layers.mask;
      const previousAutoClear = renderer.autoClear;
      const previousClearAlpha = renderer.getClearAlpha();
      renderer.getClearColor(exactOutlineSavedClear);

      try {
        renderer.autoClear = false;
        renderer.setRenderTarget(modelOutlineTarget);
        renderer.setClearColor(0x000000, 0);
        renderer.clear(true, true, true);

        // Depth-only map pass. Static triangles are merged once; the moving
        // banners and fountain are kept in two tiny dynamic batches. If baking
        // ever fails, the original full-scene depth path remains the fallback.
        camera.layers.set(0);
        if (exactOutlineOccluderMesh) {
          scene.overrideMaterial = null;
          syncExactOutlineDynamicOccluders();
          // Static map depth plus the two moving map batches share one scene and
          // one renderer submission; depth and visual results are unchanged.
          renderer.render(exactOutlineDynamicOccluderScene, camera);
        } else {
          scene.overrideMaterial = modelOutlineDepthMaterial;
          renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }
        // Preserve exact foreground/held-weapon and objective depth without
        // putting those moving meshes into the static map bake.
        camera.layers.set(MODEL_OUTLINE_DYNAMIC_OCCLUDER_LAYER);
        scene.overrideMaterial = modelOutlineDepthMaterial;
        renderer.render(scene, camera);

        // Exact animated skin mask, depth-tested against the world pass.
        // Fully occluded characters were rejected above with collider-based LOS,
        // then the shared depth buffer clips any partially hidden body pixels.
        camera.layers.set(MODEL_OUTLINE_LAYER);
        scene.overrideMaterial = modelOutlineMaskMaterial;
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
        exactOutlineMaskReady = true;
      } finally {
        restoreExactOutlineVisibilityGate();
        scene.overrideMaterial = previousOverride;
        camera.layers.mask = previousLayerMask;
        renderer.setRenderTarget(previousTarget);
        renderer.setClearColor(exactOutlineSavedClear, previousClearAlpha);
        renderer.autoClear = previousAutoClear;
      }
    }

    function renderExactModelOutlines(nowSeconds) {
      if (!ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES || !started || paused || gameOver) return;
      updateExactModelOutlineMask(nowSeconds);
      if (!exactOutlineMaskReady) return;
      const previousAutoClear = renderer.autoClear;
      const previousTarget = renderer.getRenderTarget();
      try {
        renderer.autoClear = false;
        renderer.setRenderTarget(null);
        renderer.render(modelOutlineCompositeScene, modelOutlineCompositeCamera);
      } finally {
        renderer.setRenderTarget(previousTarget);
        renderer.autoClear = previousAutoClear;
      }
    }

    if (ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES) resizeExactModelOutline();

    // ---------- Sky: gradient dome with a hot sun disc ----------
    const sky = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.SphereGeometry(270, 24, 12),
      new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
        side: THREE.BackSide, depthWrite: false, fog: false,
        uniforms: { sunDir: { value: new THREE.Vector3(18, 28, 12).normalize() } },
        vertexShader: `varying vec3 vDir; void main(){ vDir = normalize(position); gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position,1.0); }`,
        fragmentShader: `
          varying vec3 vDir; uniform vec3 sunDir;
          void main(){
            float h = clamp(vDir.y, -0.05, 1.0);
            vec3 zen = vec3(0.30, 0.52, 0.75);
            vec3 hor = vec3(0.93, 0.83, 0.66);
            vec3 col = mix(hor, zen, pow(max(h,0.0), 0.55));
            float s = max(dot(vDir, sunDir), 0.0);
            col += vec3(1.0, 0.86, 0.55) * (pow(s, 350.0) * 1.6 + pow(s, 12.0) * 0.22);
            gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
          }`
      })
    );
    scene.add(sky);

    const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcbe7ff, 0x8a7357, 1.15);
    scene.add(hemi);
    const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffe9bf, 2.6);
    sun.position.set(18, 28, 12);
    sun.castShadow = true;
    sun.shadow.mapSize.set(lowSpec ? 512 : 1024, lowSpec ? 512 : 1024);
    sun.shadow.radius = lowSpec ? 1 : 1.5;
    sun.shadow.camera.left = -66; sun.shadow.camera.right = 66;
    sun.shadow.camera.top = 66; sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -66;
    sun.shadow.camera.near = 4; sun.shadow.camera.far = 155;
    sun.shadow.bias = -0.0006;
    scene.add(sun);

    loadingUI?.set(62, 'BUILDING THE MAP...', 'Generating procedural materials and texture maps.');
    // ---------- Shared geometry / materials ----------
    // High-resolution deterministic procedural PBR textures. Every map is generated
    // locally, remains single-file, and is stable between reloads.
    const textureAnisotropy = Math.max(1, Math.min(lowSpec ? 3 : 6, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy()));
    const SURFACE_TEX_SIZE = lowSpec ? 384 : 768;
    const DETAIL_TEX_SIZE = lowSpec ? 256 : 512;

    function seededRandom(seed) {
      let a = seed >>> 0;
      return function () {
        a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
        let t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a);
        t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
        return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
      };
    }
    function hashSeed(value) {
      const text = String(value); let h = 2166136261;
      for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) { h ^= text.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619); }
      return h >>> 0;
    }
    function clampByte(v) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(v))); }
    function parseHexColor(hex) {
      const value = Number.parseInt(String(hex).replace('#',''), 16);
      return [(value >> 16) & 255, (value >> 8) & 255, value & 255];
    }
    function shadeHex(hex, amount) {
      const [r,g,b] = parseHexColor(hex);
      return `rgb(${clampByte(r + amount)},${clampByte(g + amount)},${clampByte(b + amount)})`;
    }
    function rgbaHex(hex, alpha, amount = 0) {
      const [r,g,b] = parseHexColor(hex);
      return `rgba(${clampByte(r + amount)},${clampByte(g + amount)},${clampByte(b + amount)},${alpha})`;
    }
    function configureMapTexture(t, mirrored = false, colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace) {
      t.wrapS = t.wrapT = mirrored ? THREE.MirroredRepeatWrapping : THREE.RepeatWrapping;
      t.colorSpace = colorSpace;
      t.anisotropy = textureAnisotropy;
      t.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter;
      t.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
      t.generateMipmaps = true;
      t.needsUpdate = true;
      return t;
    }
    function makeTexture(size, draw, mirrored = true, seed = 1) {
      const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = size;
      const g = c.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
      draw(g, size, seededRandom(hashSeed(seed)));
      return configureMapTexture(new THREE.CanvasTexture(c), mirrored, THREE.SRGBColorSpace);
    }
    function makeDetailTexture(size, draw, mirrored = true, seed = 1) {
      const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = size;
      const g = c.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
      draw(g, size, seededRandom(hashSeed(seed)));
      return configureMapTexture(new THREE.CanvasTexture(c), mirrored, THREE.NoColorSpace);
    }
    function makeNormalTextureFromHeight(heightTexture, strength = 2.2) {
      const source = heightTexture.image;
      const width = source.width, height = source.height;
      const src = source.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true }).getImageData(0,0,width,height).data;
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = width; canvas.height = height;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
      const image = ctx.createImageData(width,height), dst = image.data;
      for (let y=0;y<height;y++) {
        const ym = y===0 ? height-1 : y-1, yp = y===height-1 ? 0 : y+1;
        for (let x=0;x<width;x++) {
          const xm = x===0 ? width-1 : x-1, xp = x===width-1 ? 0 : x+1;
          const left = src[(y*width+xm)*4], right = src[(y*width+xp)*4];
          const up = src[(ym*width+x)*4], down = src[(yp*width+x)*4];
          let nx = (left-right)/255*strength, ny = (up-down)/255*strength, nz = 1;
          const inv = 1/Math.hypot(nx,ny,nz); nx*=inv; ny*=inv; nz*=inv;
          const i=(y*width+x)*4;
          dst[i]=clampByte((nx*.5+.5)*255); dst[i+1]=clampByte((ny*.5+.5)*255);
          dst[i+2]=clampByte((nz*.5+.5)*255); dst[i+3]=255;
        }
      }
      ctx.putImageData(image,0,0);
      const mirrored = heightTexture.wrapS === THREE.MirroredRepeatWrapping;
      const normal = configureMapTexture(new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas), mirrored, THREE.NoColorSpace);
      normal.repeat.copy(heightTexture.repeat);
      return normal;
    }
    function scatterSpeckle(g, s, count, rng, base, alpha, maxRadius = 3) {
      const [r,gc,b] = parseHexColor(base);
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const d = (rng() - .5) * 56;
        g.fillStyle = `rgba(${clampByte(r+d)},${clampByte(gc+d)},${clampByte(b+d)},${alpha * (.3 + rng()*.7)})`;
        const radius = .45 + rng() * maxRadius;
        g.fillRect(rng()*s, rng()*s, radius, radius);
      }
    }
    function softStain(g, x, y, radius, color) {
      const grad = g.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, radius);
      grad.addColorStop(0, color); grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
      g.fillStyle = grad; g.fillRect(x-radius, y-radius, radius*2, radius*2);
    }

    const tex = {
      plaster: (base) => makeTexture(SURFACE_TEX_SIZE, (g,s,rng) => {
        const grad = g.createLinearGradient(0,0,s,s);
        grad.addColorStop(0, shadeHex(base, 12)); grad.addColorStop(.48, base); grad.addColorStop(1, shadeHex(base, -9));
        g.fillStyle = grad; g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);
        scatterSpeckle(g,s,Math.floor(s*s/145),rng,base,.12,2.6);
        for(let i=0;i<18;i++) softStain(g,rng()*s,rng()*s,(.035+rng()*.1)*s,rgbaHex(base,.08,-55-rng()*25));
        for(let i=0;i<14;i++){
          let x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s*.85;
          g.strokeStyle=`rgba(54,43,34,${.12+rng()*.18})`;g.lineWidth=.7+rng()*1.15;
          g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);
          const branches=4+Math.floor(rng()*6);
          for(let k=0;k<branches;k++){x+=(rng()-.5)*s*.045;y+=rng()*s*.035;g.lineTo(x,y);}g.stroke();
        }
        // Irregular sun-bleached/chipped patches add scale without obvious repetition.
        for(let i=0;i<24;i++){
          const x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s,w=(.012+rng()*.055)*s,h=(.006+rng()*.025)*s;
          g.fillStyle=rgbaHex(base,.08+rng()*.09,18+rng()*20);g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,y,w,h,rng()*Math.PI,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
        }
        // Fine lime grains, pinholes and rain streaks survive close inspection.
        for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(s*s/460);i++){
          const light=rng()>.48; g.fillStyle=rgbaHex(base,.10+rng()*.13,light?28:-30);
          const r=.35+rng()*1.25; g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,r,r);
        }
        for(let i=0;i<34;i++){
          const x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s*.65,len=(.025+rng()*.16)*s;
          const streak=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x,y+len);streak.addColorStop(0,'rgba(64,47,33,.11)');streak.addColorStop(1,'rgba(64,47,33,0)');
          g.fillStyle=streak;g.fillRect(x,y,.7+rng()*2.1,len);
        }
      }, true, `plaster-${base}`),

      stone: (base) => makeTexture(SURFACE_TEX_SIZE, (g,s,rng) => {
        const rows=8, cols=6, bh=s/rows, bw=s/cols;
        g.fillStyle='#625b51';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);
        for(let row=0;row<rows;row++){
          const offset=(row&1)*bw*.5;
          for(let col=-1;col<=cols;col++){
            const x=col*bw-offset+2,y=row*bh+2,w=bw-4,h=bh-4;
            const delta=(rng()-.5)*25;
            const blockGrad=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x+w,y+h);
            blockGrad.addColorStop(0,shadeHex(base,delta+10));blockGrad.addColorStop(1,shadeHex(base,delta-12));
            g.fillStyle=blockGrad;g.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
            for(let i=0;i<18;i++){const v=105+rng()*90|0;g.fillStyle=`rgba(${v},${v-8},${v-18},.08)`;const r=.7+rng()*2.2;g.fillRect(x+rng()*w,y+rng()*h,r,r);}
            g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,244,220,.12)';g.lineWidth=1;g.strokeRect(x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);
            if(rng()>.52){g.fillStyle='rgba(52,45,38,.14)';g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x+rng()*w,y+rng()*h,2+rng()*8,1+rng()*3,rng()*Math.PI,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();}
          }
        }
        g.strokeStyle='rgba(48,43,38,.38)';g.lineWidth=Math.max(3,s/210);
        for(let y=0;y<=s;y+=bh){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,y);g.lineTo(s,y);g.stroke();}
        for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(s*s/510);i++){
          const v=75+rng()*120|0;g.fillStyle=`rgba(${v},${v-7},${v-15},${.10+rng()*.17})`;
          const r=.35+rng()*1.7;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,r,r);
        }
        for(let i=0;i<42;i++){
          let x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s;g.strokeStyle=`rgba(46,40,34,${.09+rng()*.13})`;g.lineWidth=.45+rng()*.8;
          g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);for(let k=0;k<3+Math.floor(rng()*5);k++){x+=(rng()-.5)*s*.025;y+=(rng()-.5)*s*.018;g.lineTo(x,y);}g.stroke();
        }
      }, true, `stone-${base}`),

      wood: makeTexture(SURFACE_TEX_SIZE, (g,s,rng) => {
        const plankH=s/5;
        for(let row=0;row<5;row++){
          const y=row*plankH, base=row%2?'#8a6039':'#956b43';
          const grad=g.createLinearGradient(0,y,s,y+plankH);
          grad.addColorStop(0,shadeHex(base,8));grad.addColorStop(.5,base);grad.addColorStop(1,shadeHex(base,-13));
          g.fillStyle=grad;g.fillRect(0,y,s,plankH);
          for(let i=0;i<48;i++){
            const gy=y+rng()*plankH;
            g.strokeStyle=`rgba(55,31,15,${.055+rng()*.12})`;g.lineWidth=.6+rng()*1.7;
            g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,gy);g.bezierCurveTo(s*.28,gy+(rng()-.5)*13,s*.68,gy+(rng()-.5)*15,s,gy+(rng()-.5)*7);g.stroke();
          }
          for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
            const x=(.12+rng()*.76)*s, ky=y+(.18+rng()*.64)*plankH;
            g.strokeStyle='rgba(52,29,14,.38)';g.lineWidth=2+rng()*3;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,ky,9+rng()*15,3+rng()*6,rng()*.25,0,Math.PI*2);g.stroke();
          }
        }
        g.strokeStyle='rgba(35,24,16,.62)';g.lineWidth=Math.max(3,s/170);
        for(let y=0;y<=s;y+=plankH){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,y);g.lineTo(s,y);g.stroke();}
        g.fillStyle='rgba(25,23,20,.78)';
        for(let y=plankH*.5;y<s;y+=plankH)for(const x of [s*.045,s*.955]){g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,3+s/420,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();}
        for(let i=0;i<210;i++){
          const x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s,len=(.008+rng()*.075)*s;
          g.strokeStyle=`rgba(238,203,154,${.035+rng()*.075})`;g.lineWidth=.35+rng()*.75;
          g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);g.lineTo(Math.min(s,x+len),y+(rng()-.5)*2.2);g.stroke();
        }
        for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(s*s/760);i++){g.fillStyle=`rgba(45,25,13,${.06+rng()*.11})`;const r=.3+rng()*1.15;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,r,r);}
      }, true, 'warm-market-wood'),

      stripes: (a,b) => makeTexture(SURFACE_TEX_SIZE, (g,s,rng) => {
        const stripe=s/10;
        for(let x=0;x<s;x+=stripe){
          const base=((x/stripe)|0)%2?a:b;
          const grad=g.createLinearGradient(x,0,x+stripe,0);
          grad.addColorStop(0,shadeHex(base,-10));grad.addColorStop(.18,base);grad.addColorStop(.82,shadeHex(base,8));grad.addColorStop(1,shadeHex(base,-12));
          g.fillStyle=grad;g.fillRect(x,0,stripe,s);
        }
        g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.10)';g.lineWidth=1;
        for(let i=0;i<s;i+=4){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,i+.5);g.lineTo(s,i+.5);g.stroke();}
        g.strokeStyle='rgba(42,33,26,.065)';for(let i=0;i<s;i+=5){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(i+.5,0);g.lineTo(i+.5,s);g.stroke();}
        for(let i=0;i<12;i++)softStain(g,rng()*s,rng()*s,(.025+rng()*.07)*s,'rgba(70,48,28,.055)');
      }, false, `awning-${a}-${b}`),

      fabric: makeTexture(SURFACE_TEX_SIZE, (g,s,rng) => {
        const grad=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,s,s);grad.addColorStop(0,'#c8b48c');grad.addColorStop(.55,'#b9a078');grad.addColorStop(1,'#aa9069');
        g.fillStyle=grad;g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);
        g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,248,224,.13)';g.lineWidth=1;for(let i=0;i<s;i+=4){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,i);g.lineTo(s,i);g.stroke();}
        g.strokeStyle='rgba(65,49,31,.10)';for(let i=0;i<s;i+=5){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(i,0);g.lineTo(i,s);g.stroke();}
        for(let i=0;i<20;i++)softStain(g,rng()*s,rng()*s,(.02+rng()*.06)*s,'rgba(67,48,28,.06)');
        for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(s/2);i++){
          const x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s;g.strokeStyle=`rgba(255,247,218,${.025+rng()*.055})`;g.lineWidth=.3+rng()*.55;
          g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);g.lineTo(x+(rng()-.5)*12,y+(rng()-.5)*3);g.stroke();
        }
      }, true, 'woven-sack-cloth'),

      metal: makeTexture(SURFACE_TEX_SIZE, (g,s,rng) => {
        const grad=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,s,0);grad.addColorStop(0,'#505b60');grad.addColorStop(.48,'#768186');grad.addColorStop(1,'#465156');
        g.fillStyle=grad;g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);
        for(let x=0;x<s;x+=2){const v=80+Math.floor(rng()*70);g.fillStyle=`rgba(${v},${v+5},${v+8},${.04+rng()*.09})`;g.fillRect(x,0,1,s);}
        for(let i=0;i<28;i++)softStain(g,rng()*s,rng()*s,(.008+rng()*.035)*s,'rgba(126,62,27,.18)');
        for(let i=0;i<240;i++){
          const x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s,len=(.006+rng()*.095)*s;
          g.strokeStyle=rng()>.38?`rgba(224,235,238,${.035+rng()*.095})`:`rgba(35,42,44,${.05+rng()*.11})`;
          g.lineWidth=.35+rng()*.7;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);g.lineTo(Math.min(s,x+len),y+(rng()-.5)*2);g.stroke();
        }
        for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(s*s/850);i++){g.fillStyle=`rgba(72,42,25,${.06+rng()*.16})`;const r=.35+rng()*1.4;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,r,r);}
      }, true, 'aged-brushed-metal')
    };

    const detailTex = {
      plaster: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{
        g.fillStyle='#929292';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);
        for(let i=0;i<s*s/70;i++){const v=104+rng()*82|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;const r=.5+rng()*2.8;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,r,r);}
        g.strokeStyle='rgba(52,52,52,.62)';g.lineWidth=1;for(let i=0;i<16;i++){let x=rng()*s,y=rng()*s;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);for(let k=0;k<6;k++){x+=(rng()-.5)*s*.05;y+=rng()*s*.035;g.lineTo(x,y);}g.stroke();}
      },true,'plaster-bump'),
      stone: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{
        const rows=8,cols=6,bh=s/rows,bw=s/cols;g.fillStyle='#626262';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);
        for(let row=0;row<rows;row++){const off=(row&1)*bw*.5;for(let col=-1;col<=cols;col++){const v=142+rng()*45|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(col*bw-off+3,row*bh+3,bw-6,bh-6);}}
        for(let i=0;i<s*s/120;i++){const v=105+rng()*90|0;g.fillStyle=`rgba(${v},${v},${v},.5)`;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,1+rng()*4,1+rng()*4);}
      },true,'stone-bump'),
      wood: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{
        g.fillStyle='#8d8d8d';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);const ph=s/5;
        for(let i=0;i<s*s/165;i++){const v=65+rng()*120|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,5+rng()*55,1+rng()*2);}
        g.strokeStyle='#454545';g.lineWidth=4;for(let y=0;y<=s;y+=ph){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,y);g.lineTo(s,y);g.stroke();}
      },true,'wood-bump'),
      fabric: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s)=>{
        g.fillStyle='#888';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);g.strokeStyle='#a7a7a7';g.lineWidth=1;for(let i=0;i<s;i+=4){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,i);g.lineTo(s,i);g.stroke();}
        g.strokeStyle='#696969';for(let i=0;i<s;i+=5){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(i,0);g.lineTo(i,s);g.stroke();}
      },true,'fabric-bump'),
      metal: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{
        g.fillStyle='#888';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);for(let x=0;x<s;x++){const v=100+rng()*70|0;g.fillStyle=`rgba(${v},${v},${v},.28)`;g.fillRect(x,0,1,s);}
      },true,'metal-bump')
    };
    const roughTex = {
      plaster: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{g.fillStyle='#e3e3e3';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);for(let i=0;i<s*s/100;i++){const v=185+rng()*65|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,1+rng()*5,1+rng()*5);}},true,'plaster-rough'),
      stone: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{g.fillStyle='#d7d7d7';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);for(let i=0;i<s*s/130;i++){const v=165+rng()*80|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,1+rng()*6,1+rng()*6);}},true,'stone-rough'),
      wood: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{g.fillStyle='#c4c4c4';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);for(let i=0;i<s*s/145;i++){const v=110+rng()*115|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,5+rng()*45,1+rng()*2);}},true,'wood-rough'),
      fabric: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s)=>{g.fillStyle='#f1f1f1';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);g.strokeStyle='#d7d7d7';for(let i=0;i<s;i+=4){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,i);g.lineTo(s,i);g.stroke();}},true,'fabric-rough'),
      metal: makeDetailTexture(DETAIL_TEX_SIZE,(g,s,rng)=>{g.fillStyle='#8f8f8f';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);for(let i=0;i<s*s/190;i++){const v=70+rng()*120|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,1+rng()*18,1);}},true,'metal-rough')
    };
    const roughForDetail = new Map([
      [detailTex.plaster,roughTex.plaster],[detailTex.stone,roughTex.stone],[detailTex.wood,roughTex.wood],
      [detailTex.fabric,roughTex.fabric],[detailTex.metal,roughTex.metal]
    ]);

    const normalForDetail = new Map([
      [detailTex.plaster,makeNormalTextureFromHeight(detailTex.plaster,lowSpec?1.45:2.45)],
      [detailTex.stone,makeNormalTextureFromHeight(detailTex.stone,lowSpec?1.7:2.9)],
      [detailTex.wood,makeNormalTextureFromHeight(detailTex.wood,lowSpec?1.4:2.35)],
      [detailTex.fabric,makeNormalTextureFromHeight(detailTex.fabric,lowSpec?1.1:1.75)],
      [detailTex.metal,makeNormalTextureFromHeight(detailTex.metal,lowSpec?1.15:1.9)]
    ]);

    const texturedMat = (map, color = 0xffffff, roughness = .88, tileSize = 2.5, detailMap = null, bumpScale = .07, metalness = 0) => {
      const normalMap = detailMap ? normalForDetail.get(detailMap) || null : null;
      const normalStrength = detailMap ? Math.min(1.35,.48+bumpScale*7.2) : 0;
      const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        map, color, roughness, metalness,
        bumpMap: detailMap || null,
        bumpScale: detailMap ? bumpScale*.28 : 0,
        normalMap,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(normalStrength,normalStrength),
        roughnessMap: detailMap ? roughForDetail.get(detailMap) || null : null
      });
      material.dithering = true;
      material.userData.tileSize = tileSize;
      return material;
    };

    const mat = (color, roughness = .82, metalness = .025) => {
      const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness, metalness, flatShading:false });
      material.dithering=true; return material;
    };
    const colors = {
      sand:0xc9ae82, plaster:0xd8cfba, dark:0x30313a, red:0xb94b46,
      blue:0x487a91, teal:0x4c8d84, crate:0x8b653d, floor:0xa89474,
      fish:0x70b6c9, gold:0xf3c552
    };
    const materials = {
      floor:mat(colors.floor),
      sand:texturedMat(tex.stone('#c9ae82'),0xffffff,.94,2.2,detailTex.stone,.11),
      plaster:texturedMat(tex.plaster('#d8cfba'),0xffffff,.94,2.7,detailTex.plaster,.065),
      dark:texturedMat(tex.metal,0x62686b,.64,1.35,detailTex.metal,.028,.34),
      red:texturedMat(tex.plaster('#b94b46'),0xffffff,.92,2.7,detailTex.plaster,.065),
      blue:texturedMat(tex.plaster('#487a91'),0xffffff,.92,2.7,detailTex.plaster,.065),
      teal:texturedMat(tex.plaster('#4c8d84'),0xffffff,.92,2.7,detailTex.plaster,.065),
      crate:texturedMat(tex.wood,0xffffff,.83,1.35,detailTex.wood,.095),
      fish:new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({color:colors.fish,roughness:.28,clearcoat:.45,clearcoatRoughness:.32}),
      gold:new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:colors.gold,roughness:.3,metalness:.62}),
      black:mat(0x15161a,.54,.18),white:mat(0xf1eee5,.72),pink:mat(0xf0a5a8,.66),
      eye:new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x151515})
    };
    const awningRed=texturedMat(tex.stripes('#c8564f','#f1e8d8'),0xffffff,.96,1.05,detailTex.fabric,.027);
    const awningBlue=texturedMat(tex.stripes('#4c85a0','#f1e8d8'),0xffffff,.96,1.05,detailTex.fabric,.027);

    function mesh(geometry, material, cast = false, receive = true) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
      m.castShadow = cast;
      m.receiveShadow = receive;
      return m;
    }

    // ---------- Map ----------
    const MAP_HALF = 52;
    const MAP_SIZE = MAP_HALF * 2;
    const MAP_RENDER_SIZE = MAP_SIZE + 12;
    const PROJECTILE_LIMIT = MAP_HALF + 3;
    const colliders = [];
    // Static collider broad phase. The grid stores the original AABB objects, so
    // narrow-phase math, hit distances, movement boundaries, and map behavior stay
    // byte-for-byte equivalent while most queries inspect only nearby cells.
    const COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE = 12;
    // R109: the collision map is immutable once construction finishes, and its
    // occupied cell range is tiny. Store buckets in one dense row-major array
    // instead of hashing/packing two integers through Map.get() for every movement,
    // vision, outline, and hitscan cell visit. Bucket insertion order is unchanged.
    let colliderGrid = [];
    let colliderGridMinX=0,colliderGridMinZ=0,colliderGridWidth=0,colliderGridHeight=0,colliderGridCellCount=0;
    let colliderGridReady = false;
    const MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS=.48,MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS_SQ=MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS*MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS;
    let movementDefinitelyClearCells=new Uint8Array(0),movementDefinitelyClearCellCount=0;
    let colliderQueryStamp = 0;
    function beginColliderQuery(){
      colliderQueryStamp++;
      if(colliderQueryStamp<2147483000)return colliderQueryStamp;
      for(const collider of colliders)collider._queryStamp=0;
      colliderQueryStamp=1;
      return colliderQueryStamp;
    }
    function buildColliderSpatialIndex(){
      let minGridX=Infinity,maxGridX=-Infinity,minGridZ=Infinity,maxGridZ=-Infinity;
      for(let index=0;index<colliders.length;index++){
        const collider=colliders[index];
        collider._spatialId=index;collider._queryStamp=0;
        const minCellX=Math.floor(collider.minX/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),maxCellX=Math.floor(collider.maxX/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE);
        const minCellZ=Math.floor(collider.minZ/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),maxCellZ=Math.floor(collider.maxZ/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE);
        if(minCellX<minGridX)minGridX=minCellX;if(maxCellX>maxGridX)maxGridX=maxCellX;
        if(minCellZ<minGridZ)minGridZ=minCellZ;if(maxCellZ>maxGridZ)maxGridZ=maxCellZ;
      }
      if(!colliders.length){colliderGrid=[];colliderGridWidth=colliderGridHeight=colliderGridCellCount=0;colliderGridReady=false;movementDefinitelyClearCells=new Uint8Array(0);movementDefinitelyClearCellCount=0;return;}
      colliderGridMinX=minGridX;colliderGridMinZ=minGridZ;
      colliderGridWidth=maxGridX-minGridX+1;colliderGridHeight=maxGridZ-minGridZ+1;
      colliderGrid=new Array(colliderGridWidth*colliderGridHeight);colliderGridCellCount=0;
      for(let index=0;index<colliders.length;index++){
        const collider=colliders[index];
        const minCellX=Math.floor(collider.minX/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),maxCellX=Math.floor(collider.maxX/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE);
        const minCellZ=Math.floor(collider.minZ/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),maxCellZ=Math.floor(collider.maxZ/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE);
        for(let cellX=minCellX;cellX<=maxCellX;cellX++)for(let cellZ=minCellZ;cellZ<=maxCellZ;cellZ++){
          const gridIndex=(cellZ-colliderGridMinZ)*colliderGridWidth+(cellX-colliderGridMinX);
          let bucket=colliderGrid[gridIndex];
          if(!bucket){bucket=[];colliderGrid[gridIndex]=bucket;colliderGridCellCount++;}
          bucket.push(collider);
        }
      }
      colliderGridReady=colliderGridCellCount>0;
      // Conservative movement broad-phase for the shared player/bot radius. A cell
      // is marked clear only when it does not intersect ANY collider expanded by
      // radius .48. Therefore every point in a clear cell is guaranteed unable to
      // collide, while all boundary/near-wall positions retain the original exact
      // circle-vs-AABB query below.
      movementDefinitelyClearCells=new Uint8Array(colliderGridWidth*colliderGridHeight);movementDefinitelyClearCells.fill(1);movementDefinitelyClearCellCount=movementDefinitelyClearCells.length;
      for(let index=0;index<colliders.length;index++){
        const collider=colliders[index];
        const minCellX=Math.max(colliderGridMinX,Math.floor((collider.minX-MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE));
        const maxCellX=Math.min(colliderGridMinX+colliderGridWidth-1,Math.floor((collider.maxX+MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE));
        const minCellZ=Math.max(colliderGridMinZ,Math.floor((collider.minZ-MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE));
        const maxCellZ=Math.min(colliderGridMinZ+colliderGridHeight-1,Math.floor((collider.maxZ+MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE));
        for(let cellX=minCellX;cellX<=maxCellX;cellX++)for(let cellZ=minCellZ;cellZ<=maxCellZ;cellZ++){
          const clearIndex=(cellZ-colliderGridMinZ)*colliderGridWidth+(cellX-colliderGridMinX);
          if(movementDefinitelyClearCells[clearIndex]){movementDefinitelyClearCells[clearIndex]=0;movementDefinitelyClearCellCount--;}
        }
      }
      safeConsoleInfo('[CatStrike] Collider spatial index:',colliders.length,'AABBs in',colliderGridCellCount,'cells; movement-clear cells:',movementDefinitelyClearCellCount,'/',movementDefinitelyClearCells.length);
    }
    function nearestColliderHitDistance(origin,dir,maxDist,minHeight=0){
      if(!colliderGridReady){
        let nearest=maxDist;
        for(const collider of colliders){if(collider.h<minHeight)continue;const hit=rayAABBHitDistance(origin,dir,collider,nearest);if(hit<nearest)nearest=hit;}
        return nearest<maxDist?nearest:Infinity;
      }
      const stamp=beginColliderQuery();
      let nearest=maxDist;
      let cellX=Math.floor(origin.x/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),cellZ=Math.floor(origin.z/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE);
      const endX=origin.x+dir.x*maxDist,endZ=origin.z+dir.z*maxDist;
      const endCellX=Math.floor(endX/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),endCellZ=Math.floor(endZ/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE);
      const stepX=dir.x>1e-12?1:dir.x<-1e-12?-1:0;
      const stepZ=dir.z>1e-12?1:dir.z<-1e-12?-1:0;
      const tDeltaX=stepX?Math.abs(COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE/dir.x):Infinity;
      const tDeltaZ=stepZ?Math.abs(COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE/dir.z):Infinity;
      const boundaryX=stepX>0?(cellX+1)*COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE:cellX*COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE;
      const boundaryZ=stepZ>0?(cellZ+1)*COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE:cellZ*COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE;
      let tMaxX=stepX?(boundaryX-origin.x)/dir.x:Infinity;
      let tMaxZ=stepZ?(boundaryZ-origin.z)/dir.z:Infinity;
      if(tMaxX<0)tMaxX=0;if(tMaxZ<0)tMaxZ=0;

      for(let guard=0;guard<2048;guard++){
        const gridX=cellX-colliderGridMinX,gridZ=cellZ-colliderGridMinZ;
        // R112: DDA cell coordinates are monotonic on each axis. Once traversal is
        // outside the immutable collider grid and that axis is stationary or moving
        // farther outward, no later cell can ever re-enter the grid, so continuing to
        // maxDist can only visit empty space. Rays starting outside but aimed inward
        // still traverse normally until they enter, preserving every hit and distance.
        if((gridX<0&&stepX<=0)||(gridX>=colliderGridWidth&&stepX>=0)||
           (gridZ<0&&stepZ<=0)||(gridZ>=colliderGridHeight&&stepZ>=0))break;
        const bucket=gridX>=0&&gridX<colliderGridWidth&&gridZ>=0&&gridZ<colliderGridHeight?colliderGrid[gridZ*colliderGridWidth+gridX]:undefined;
        if(bucket)for(let i=0;i<bucket.length;i++){
          const collider=bucket[i];
          if(collider._queryStamp===stamp||collider.h<minHeight)continue;
          collider._queryStamp=stamp;
          const hit=rayAABBHitDistance(origin,dir,collider,nearest);
          if(hit<nearest)nearest=hit;
        }
        const nextBoundary=Math.min(tMaxX,tMaxZ);
        if(nearest<maxDist&&nearest<=nextBoundary+1e-10)return nearest;
        if(cellX===endCellX&&cellZ===endCellZ)break;
        if(tMaxX<tMaxZ){cellX+=stepX;tMaxX+=tDeltaX;}
        else if(tMaxZ<tMaxX){cellZ+=stepZ;tMaxZ+=tDeltaZ;}
        else{cellX+=stepX;cellZ+=stepZ;tMaxX+=tDeltaX;tMaxZ+=tDeltaZ;}
        if(nextBoundary>maxDist)break;
      }
      return nearest<maxDist?nearest:Infinity;
    }
    const mapObjects = [];
    const tiledBoxGeometryCache = new Map();

    function getTiledBoxGeometry(w, h, d, tileSize = 2.5) {
      const key = `${w}|${h}|${d}|${tileSize}`;
      let geometry = tiledBoxGeometryCache.get(key);
      if (geometry) return geometry;

      geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
      const pos = geometry.attributes.position;
      const normal = geometry.attributes.normal;
      const uv = geometry.attributes.uv;
      for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
        const x = pos.getX(i), y = pos.getY(i), z = pos.getZ(i);
        const nx = Math.abs(normal.getX(i)), ny = Math.abs(normal.getY(i));
        if (nx > .5) uv.setXY(i, z / tileSize, y / tileSize);       // left/right
        else if (ny > .5) uv.setXY(i, x / tileSize, z / tileSize); // roof/floor
        else uv.setXY(i, x / tileSize, y / tileSize);              // front/back
      }
      uv.needsUpdate = true;
      geometry.computeBoundingBox();
      geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
      tiledBoxGeometryCache.set(key, geometry);
      return geometry;
    }

    function boxGeometryFor(material, w, h, d) {
      return getTiledBoxGeometry(w, h, d, material?.map ? (material.userData.tileSize || 2.5) : Math.max(w, h, d));
    }

    function addBox(x, y, z, w, h, d, material, collision = true) {
      // UVs are scaled per physical face. Long walls now tile horizontally,
      // roofs tile in X/Z, and thin edges no longer inherit a stretched front UV.
      const castShadow = !lowSpec && h >= 2.2 && (w * d) >= 7;
      const m = mesh(boxGeometryFor(material, w, h, d), material, castShadow, true);
      m.position.set(x, y + h / 2, z);
      scene.add(m);
      mapObjects.push(m);
      if (collision) colliders.push({ minX: x - w/2, maxX: x + w/2, minZ: z - d/2, maxZ: z + d/2, h });
      return m;
    }

    // Ground and boundaries — expanded 2.65× in playable area.
    const groundTex = (() => {
      const size=lowSpec?384:768;
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=size;
      const g=c.getContext('2d',{alpha:false}),rng=seededRandom(0xC4757A11);
      const rows=12,cols=12,bh=size/rows,bw=size/cols;
      g.fillStyle='#665b4c';g.fillRect(0,0,size,size);
      for(let row=0;row<rows;row++){
        const offset=(row&1)*bw*.5;
        for(let col=-1;col<=cols;col++){
          const x=col*bw-offset+2.4,y=row*bh+2.4,w=bw-4.8,h=bh-4.8;
          const base=['#a79170','#ae9874','#9f8969','#b29d79'][(row*7+col*3+4)%4];
          const delta=(rng()-.5)*18;
          const grad=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x+w,y+h);
          grad.addColorStop(0,shadeHex(base,delta+9));grad.addColorStop(.55,shadeHex(base,delta));grad.addColorStop(1,shadeHex(base,delta-12));          g.fillStyle=grad;g.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
          g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,242,215,.11)';g.lineWidth=1;g.strokeRect(x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);
          for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const v=105+rng()*85|0;g.fillStyle=`rgba(${v},${v-10},${v-28},.08)`;const r=1+rng()*3;g.fillRect(x+rng()*w,y+rng()*h,r,r);}
          if(rng()>.67){g.fillStyle='rgba(62,48,34,.13)';g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x+rng()*w,y+rng()*h,3+rng()*9,1+rng()*4,rng()*Math.PI,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();}
        }
      }
      // Subtle polished traffic paths, dust blooms, and stains keep the plaza from reading as a tiled grid.
      g.strokeStyle='rgba(235,211,169,.055)';g.lineWidth=size*.035;
      for(const y of [size*.29,size*.51,size*.73]){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-20,y);g.bezierCurveTo(size*.28,y-size*.025,size*.72,y+size*.02,size+20,y-size*.01);g.stroke();}
      for(let i=0;i<38;i++)softStain(g,rng()*size,rng()*size,(.012+rng()*.045)*size,'rgba(63,45,29,.07)');
      const t=configureMapTexture(new THREE.CanvasTexture(c),true,THREE.SRGBColorSpace);t.repeat.set(13,13);return t;
    })();
    const groundBump=makeDetailTexture(lowSpec?256:512,(g,s,rng)=>{
      const rows=12,cols=12,bh=s/rows,bw=s/cols;g.fillStyle='#555';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);
      for(let row=0;row<rows;row++){const off=(row&1)*bw*.5;for(let col=-1;col<=cols;col++){const v=135+rng()*45|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(col*bw-off+3,row*bh+3,bw-6,bh-6);}}
      for(let i=0;i<s*s/105;i++){const v=92+rng()*105|0;g.fillStyle=`rgba(${v},${v},${v},.55)`;const r=1+rng()*4;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,r,r);}
    },true,'paving-bump');groundBump.repeat.set(13,13);
    const groundNormal=makeNormalTextureFromHeight(groundBump,lowSpec?1.55:2.75);groundNormal.repeat.set(13,13);
    const groundRough=makeDetailTexture(lowSpec?256:512,(g,s,rng)=>{
      g.fillStyle='#dddddd';g.fillRect(0,0,s,s);for(let i=0;i<s*s/115;i++){const v=145+rng()*105|0;g.fillStyle=`rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;g.fillRect(rng()*s,rng()*s,1+rng()*7,1+rng()*7);}
    },true,'paving-rough');groundRough.repeat.set(13,13);
    const ground=mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(MAP_RENDER_SIZE,MAP_RENDER_SIZE,1,1),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({map:groundTex,bumpMap:groundBump,bumpScale:.026,normalMap:groundNormal,normalScale:new THREE.Vector2(1.05,1.05),roughnessMap:groundRough,roughness:.96,metalness:0}),
      false,true
    );
    ground.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;scene.add(ground);

    // Tall perimeter architecture hides the hard edge and gives the arena a dense city silhouette.
    addBox(0,0,-MAP_HALF,MAP_SIZE,8,1.4,materials.plaster);
    addBox(0,0, MAP_HALF,MAP_SIZE,8,1.4,materials.plaster);
    addBox(-MAP_HALF,0,0,1.4,8,MAP_SIZE,materials.plaster);
    addBox( MAP_HALF,0,0,1.4,8,MAP_SIZE,materials.plaster);
    // Market lanes
    addBox(0, 0, 0, 3.2, 4.5, 18, materials.plaster);
    addBox(-13, 0, -10, 11, 3.8, 2.3, materials.red);
    addBox(14, 0, 9, 12, 3.8, 2.3, materials.blue);
    addBox(-14, 0, 11, 3, 5.5, 12, materials.sand);
    addBox(14, 0, -12, 3, 5.5, 11, materials.sand);
    addBox(-23, 0, -2, 8, 4.5, 3, materials.teal);
    addBox(23, 0, 2, 8, 4.5, 3, materials.teal);
    addBox(-5, 0, 22, 12, 3.6, 2.2, materials.plaster);
    addBox(7, 0, -22, 12, 3.6, 2.2, materials.plaster);

    // Crate clusters
    [
      [-7,-8,2,2,2], [-9,-8,2,3.5,2], [-22,16,3,2.4,3], [-20,18,2,2,2],
      [20,-19,3,2.4,3], [23,-17,2,2,2], [8,18,2.5,2.5,2.5], [10,20,2,2,2],
      [-16,-21,3,2.5,3], [-18,-18.5,2,2,2], [21,15,3.5,2.5,2.5]
    ].forEach(([x,z,w,h,d]) => addBox(x,0,z,w,h,d,materials.crate));

    // ---------- Building dressing: awnings, windows, doors, roof trim ----------
    const windowMat=new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({color:0x26343d,roughness:.12,metalness:.16,clearcoat:1,clearcoatRoughness:.16,emissive:0x07131a,emissiveIntensity:.22});
    const frameMat = mat(0x6e5335);
    function addDecoBox(x, y, z, w, h, d, material, ry = 0) {
      const m = mesh(boxGeometryFor(material, w, h, d), material);
      m.position.set(x, y, z); m.rotation.y = ry;
      scene.add(m); mapObjects.push(m);
      return m;
    }
    const awningScallopGeometry=new THREE.ConeGeometry(.24,.3,4);
    function addAwning(x, y, z, w, material, ry = 0) {
      const a = mesh(boxGeometryFor(material, w, .09, 1.7), material);
      a.position.set(x, y, z); a.rotation.set(.42, ry, 0);
      scene.add(a); mapObjects.push(a);
      // scalloped front edge
      for (let i = -w/2 + .5; i < w/2; i += 1) {
        const s = mesh(awningScallopGeometry, material, true, false);
        s.position.set(x + Math.cos(ry) * i, y - .34, z + .74 * Math.cos(ry) - Math.sin(ry) * i);
        s.rotation.x = Math.PI;
        scene.add(s); mapObjects.push(s);
      }
    }
    function addWindow(x, y, z, ry = 0) {
      addDecoBox(x, y, z, 1.05, 1.3, .1, frameMat, ry);
      addDecoBox(x + Math.sin(ry) * .04, y, z + Math.cos(ry) * .04, .85, 1.1, .1, windowMat, ry);
      // sill
      addDecoBox(x + Math.sin(ry) * .09, y - .72, z + Math.cos(ry) * .09, 1.2, .12, .26, frameMat, ry);
    }
    // Every door uses identical PBR inputs. Reuse one material so static batching
    // can collapse all doors instead of treating each as a unique render state.
    const doorMat = texturedMat(tex.wood, 0xffffff, .86, 1.15, detailTex.wood, .09);
    function addDoor(x, y, z, ry = 0) {
      addDecoBox(x, y + .95, z, 1.2, 1.9, .12, doorMat, ry);
      addDecoBox(x, y + 2.05, z, 1.5, .22, .2, frameMat, ry);
    }
    // Central bazaar building (0,0): doors + windows + roof ledge + gold trim
    addDoor(0, 0, 9.06); addDoor(0, 0, -9.06, Math.PI);

    addWindow(1.66, 2.6, 5, Math.PI/2); addWindow(1.66, 2.6, 0, Math.PI/2); addWindow(1.66, 2.6, -5, Math.PI/2);

    // Sand towers: stone trim, windows, crenellation hints
    for (const [tx, tz, th] of [[-14, 11, 5.5], [14, -12, 5.5]]) {
      addDecoBox(tx, th + .18, tz, 3.5, .36, 3.5, frameMat);
      addWindow(tx, th - 1.6, tz + (tz > 0 ? 1.56 : -1.56), tz > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI);
      addWindow(tx + (tx > 0 ? 1.56 : -1.56), th - 1.6, tz, Math.PI/2);
    }
    // Teal shops: windows + wood roof ledges
    addWindow(-23, 2.6, -.44, 0); addWindow(23, 2.6, 2.56, 0);
    addDecoBox(-23, 4.6, -2, 8.4, .26, 3.4, frameMat);
    addDecoBox(23, 4.6, 2, 8.4, .26, 3.4, frameMat);
    // String lights across the main lane (pure decoration, emissive dots)
    const bulbMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffd98a });
    const stringLightGeometry=new THREE.SphereGeometry(.09,6,4);
    const stringLights=new THREE.InstancedMesh(stringLightGeometry,bulbMat,13);
    stringLights.castShadow=false;stringLights.receiveShadow=false;
    const stringLightMatrixDummy=new THREE.Object3D();
    for (let i = 0; i <= 12; i++) {
      const t = i / 12;
      stringLightMatrixDummy.position.set(-14 + t * 28, 4.6 - Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * .9 + .9, -6);
      stringLightMatrixDummy.rotation.set(0,0,0);stringLightMatrixDummy.scale.set(1,1,1);stringLightMatrixDummy.updateMatrix();
      stringLights.setMatrixAt(i,stringLightMatrixDummy.matrix);
    }
    stringLights.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;stringLights.computeBoundingBox();stringLights.computeBoundingSphere();scene.add(stringLights);

    // Decorative fish signs. Reuse the three immutable primitive buffers across
    // every sign; only transforms differ, so BatchedMesh / spatial fallback does
    // not need to copy nine equivalent geometry allocations.
    const fishBodyGeometry=new THREE.SphereGeometry(.72,8,5);
    const fishTailGeometry=new THREE.ConeGeometry(.55,.8,3);
    const fishEyeGeometry=new THREE.SphereGeometry(.08,6,4);
    function addFishSign(x, y, z, ry = 0) {
      const g = new THREE.Group();
      const body = mesh(fishBodyGeometry, materials.fish);
      body.scale.set(1.5, .65, .28);
      g.add(body);
      const tail = mesh(fishTailGeometry, materials.fish);
      tail.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
      tail.position.x = -1.25;
      g.add(tail);
      const eye = mesh(fishEyeGeometry, materials.eye, false, false);
      eye.position.set(.7,.12,.28);
      g.add(eye);
      g.position.set(x,y,z); g.rotation.y = ry;
      scene.add(g);
    }
    addFishSign(0, 5.8, -8.8, 0);

    // Ground markings
    const ring = mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(3.8, 4.1, 32), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color: 0xf2c555, side: THREE.DoubleSide}), false, false);
    ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI/2; ring.position.y = .012; scene.add(ring);

    // Objective geometry is created once and only shown in bomb modes.
    const objectiveVisuals = new THREE.Group();
    objectiveVisuals.visible = false;
    scene.add(objectiveVisuals);
    const siteVisuals = {};
    const bombSiteDiscGeometry=new THREE.CircleGeometry(4.15,48);
    const bombSiteOutlineGeometry=new THREE.RingGeometry(3.85,4.18,48);
    function createBombSiteVisual(label, x, z, color) {
      const group = new THREE.Group();
      const disc = mesh(bombSiteDiscGeometry, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color, transparent:true, opacity:.09, side:THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite:false}), false, false);
      disc.rotation.x = -Math.PI/2; disc.position.y = .022; group.add(disc);
      const outline = mesh(bombSiteOutlineGeometry, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color, transparent:true, opacity:.82, side:THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite:false}), false, false);
      outline.rotation.x = -Math.PI/2; outline.position.y = .03; group.add(outline);
      const markerCanvas = document.createElement('canvas'); markerCanvas.width = markerCanvas.height = 256;
      const mg = markerCanvas.getContext('2d'); mg.fillStyle = 'rgba(12,14,18,.82)'; mg.beginPath(); mg.arc(128,128,102,0,Math.PI*2); mg.fill();
      mg.strokeStyle = '#' + color.toString(16).padStart(6,'0'); mg.lineWidth = 16; mg.stroke(); mg.fillStyle = '#fff7dd'; mg.font = '900 150px system-ui'; mg.textAlign='center'; mg.textBaseline='middle'; mg.fillText(label,128,139);
      const sprite = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:configureMapTexture(new THREE.CanvasTexture(markerCanvas)),transparent:true,depthWrite:false}));
      sprite.position.set(0,2.8,0); sprite.scale.set(2.25,2.25,1); group.add(sprite);

    // Detailed market props. Shared geometry + instancing keeps draw calls controlled.
    function makeInstanced(geometry,material,transforms,cast=true,receive=true){
      const inst=new THREE.InstancedMesh(geometry,material,transforms.length);inst.castShadow=cast;inst.receiveShadow=receive;
      const d=new THREE.Object3D();transforms.forEach((p,i)=>{d.position.set(p[0],p[1],p[2]);d.rotation.set(p[3]||0,p[4]||0,p[5]||0);const sc=p[6]||1;if(Array.isArray(sc))d.scale.set(sc[0],sc[1],sc[2]);else d.scale.setScalar(sc);d.updateMatrix();inst.setMatrixAt(i,d.matrix);});
      inst.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;scene.add(inst);return inst;
    }
    const barrelMat=texturedMat(tex.wood,0x906641,.78,.95,detailTex.wood,.09,.05);

    // R174 dense set-dressing pass. Hundreds of additional visible pieces are
    // represented by a small number of InstancedMesh draws. They are deliberately
    // non-colliding and placed against stalls/buildings/roofs so weapon traces, bot
    // routes and the proven combat geometry remain exactly as R173. Tiny decorative
    // props also do not belong in the expensive exact outline occluder bake.
    function makeDecorativeInstanced(geometry,material,transforms,cast=false,receive=true){
      const inst=makeInstanced(geometry,material,transforms,cast,receive);
      inst.userData.skipExactOutlineOccluder=true;
      return inst;
    }

    // Wooden carts with wheels and fish baskets. Geometry is immutable, so all

    function addCart(x,z,ry=0){

    }

    // Palm trees and planters around the plazas. All palms use the same two
    // primitive buffers; per-tree size is carried by the instance matrix. This is
    // exactly the former geometry under uniform scaling but replaces 60 Mesh/Group
    // scene nodes with two instanced nodes and avoids duplicate geometry uploads.
    const leafMat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x3f7f50,roughness:.82,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
    const palmTrunkMat=mat(0x745335,.9);
    const palmTrunkGeometry=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.18,.34,4.7,9);
    const palmLeafGeometry=new THREE.ConeGeometry(.42,3.2,5);
    const palmSpecs=[];
    function addPalm(x,z,scale=1){
      palmSpecs.push([x,z,scale]);
      colliders.push({minX:x-.35*scale,maxX:x+.35*scale,minZ:z-.35*scale,maxZ:z+.35*scale,h:4.8*scale});
    }
    [[-27,-39,.9],[27,-39,.9],[-27,39,.9],[27,39,.9],[-45,-8,.78],[45,8,.78]].forEach(p=>addPalm(...p));
    {
      const trunks=new THREE.InstancedMesh(palmTrunkGeometry,palmTrunkMat,palmSpecs.length);
      trunks.castShadow=false;trunks.receiveShadow=true;
      const leaves=new THREE.InstancedMesh(palmLeafGeometry,leafMat,palmSpecs.length*8);
      leaves.castShadow=true;leaves.receiveShadow=false;
      const d=new THREE.Object3D();let leafIndex=0;
      palmSpecs.forEach(([x,z,scale],treeIndex)=>{
        d.position.set(x,2.35*scale,z);d.rotation.set(0,0,0);d.scale.setScalar(scale);d.updateMatrix();trunks.setMatrixAt(treeIndex,d.matrix);
        for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
          const angle=i*Math.PI/4;
          d.position.set(x+Math.cos(angle)*1.15*scale,4.75*scale,z+Math.sin(angle)*1.15*scale);
          d.rotation.set(0,angle,Math.PI/2-.23);d.scale.set(.45*scale,scale,.16*scale);d.updateMatrix();leaves.setMatrixAt(leafIndex++,d.matrix);
        }
      });
      trunks.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;leaves.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
      trunks.computeBoundingBox();trunks.computeBoundingSphere();leaves.computeBoundingBox();leaves.computeBoundingSphere();
      scene.add(trunks,leaves);
    }
    // All collision geometry is now final; build once and reuse for movement,

    // Hanging banner lines and warm lanterns. R177 keeps every flag and its
    // independent sway phase but submits every flag through one color-instanced
    // batch instead of 22 individual Mesh draws. Geometry, placement and animation are

    const animatedBannerBatches=[];
