    const bannerFlagGeometry=new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.72,.92);
    // Flag materials differ only by base color. Instance colors reproduce those
    // exact diffuse colors with one shared Standard material and one draw batch.
    const bannerFlagMaterial=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffffff,roughness:.9,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
    function addBannerLine(ax,az,bx,bz,y,count,colors){
      const mx=(ax+bx)/2,my=y-.55,mz=(az+bz)/2;
      bannerCablePoints.push(new THREE.Vector3(ax,y,az),new THREE.Vector3(mx,my,mz),new THREE.Vector3(mx,my,mz),new THREE.Vector3(bx,y,bz));
      for(let i=1;i<count;i++){
        const t=i/count;
        const phase=i*.71;
        animatedBanners.push({
          x:THREE.MathUtils.lerp(ax,bx,t),y:y-.42-Math.sin(t*Math.PI)*.25,z:THREE.MathUtils.lerp(az,bz,t),
          ry:Math.atan2(bx-ax,bz-az),base:0,phase,sinPhase:Math.sin(phase),cosPhase:Math.cos(phase),color:colors[i%colors.length],batch:null,batchIndex:-1
        });
      }
    }
    addBannerLine(-46,-27,46,-27,7.2,12,[0xc64f48,0xe8cf86,0x4b8296]);
    addBannerLine(-46,27,46,27,7.2,12,[0x4b8296,0xe8cf86,0x4c8d69]);
    // Both sagging cables use one LineSegments submission; point pairs reproduce
    // the exact two segments of each former THREE.Line polyline.
    const bannerCableGeometry=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(bannerCablePoints);
    const bannerCables=new THREE.LineSegments(bannerCableGeometry,bannerCableMat);
    bannerCables.matrixAutoUpdate=false;scene.add(bannerCables);
    {
      const batch=new THREE.InstancedMesh(bannerFlagGeometry,bannerFlagMaterial,animatedBanners.length);
      batch.name='CatStrike.BannerFlags';
      batch.castShadow=false;batch.receiveShadow=false;
      batch.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      batch.userData.skipExactOutlineOccluder=true;
      const dummy=new THREE.Object3D();
      for(let i=0;i<animatedBanners.length;i++){
        const b=animatedBanners[i];b.batch=batch;b.batchIndex=i;
        dummy.position.set(b.x,b.y,b.z);dummy.rotation.set(0,b.ry,b.base);dummy.scale.set(1,1,1);dummy.updateMatrix();
        batch.setMatrixAt(i,dummy.matrix);
        batch.setColorAt(i,new THREE.Color(b.color));
      }
      batch.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
      if(batch.instanceColor)batch.instanceColor.needsUpdate=true;
      batch.computeBoundingBox();batch.computeBoundingSphere();
      scene.add(batch);animatedBannerBatches.push(batch);
    }
    const bannerMatrixDummy=new THREE.Object3D();
    function updateAnimatedBannerMatrices(now){
      // Angle addition replaces one transcendental call per banner with two total
      // trig calls for the entire batch. The waveform/phase is mathematically the
      // same: sin(t+p) = sin(t)cos(p) + cos(t)sin(p).
      const phaseNow=now*2,sinNow=Math.sin(phaseNow),cosNow=Math.cos(phaseNow);
      for(const b of animatedBanners){
        bannerMatrixDummy.position.set(b.x,b.y,b.z);
        bannerMatrixDummy.rotation.set(0,b.ry,b.base+(sinNow*b.cosPhase+cosNow*b.sinPhase)*.07);
        bannerMatrixDummy.scale.set(1,1,1);bannerMatrixDummy.updateMatrix();
        b.batch.setMatrixAt(b.batchIndex,bannerMatrixDummy.matrix);
      }
      for(const batch of animatedBannerBatches)batch.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
    }
    const lanterns=[];
    const lanternBulbGeometry=new THREE.SphereGeometry(.16,8,6);
    function addLantern(x,y,z){const bulb=new THREE.Mesh(lanternBulbGeometry,new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffc66b,emissive:0xff9b32,emissiveIntensity:2.2,roughness:.38}));bulb.position.set(x,y,z);scene.add(bulb);const phase=Math.random()*6;lanterns.push({m:bulb,phase,sinPhase:Math.sin(phase),cosPhase:Math.cos(phase)});}
    for(const [x,z] of [[-42,-28],[-14,-28],[14,-28],[42,-28],[-42,28],[-14,28],[14,28],[42,28]])addLantern(x,6.65,z);

    // Distant city and mountain silhouettes beyond the collision walls.
    const skylineMat=mat(0x8f7e66,.97),mountainMat=mat(0x756c61,1);
    const skylineTransforms=[];
    for(let i=0;i<28;i++){
      const a=i/28*Math.PI*2,r=66+Math.sin(i*12.37)*4,x=Math.cos(a)*r,z=Math.sin(a)*r;
      const w=5+((i*7)%8),h=7+((i*11)%14),d=5+((i*5)%8);
      skylineTransforms.push([x,h/2-1.5,z,0,-a+Math.PI/2,0,[w,h,d]]);
    }
    makeInstanced(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1,1,1),skylineMat,skylineTransforms,false,false);
    const mountainTransforms=[];
    for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const a=i/12*Math.PI*2,r=92+Math.sin(i*2.1)*10,rad=13+(i%4)*4,h=25+(i%5)*5;mountainTransforms.push([Math.cos(a)*r,8,Math.sin(a)*r,0,a,0,[rad,h,rad]]);}
    makeInstanced(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1,1,7),mountainMat,mountainTransforms,false,false);

    // Atmospheric dust motes: one draw call, low density on weak devices.
    const dustCount=lowSpec?48:120,dustPos=new Float32Array(dustCount*3),dustSeedSin=new Float64Array(dustCount),dustSeedCos=new Float64Array(dustCount);
    for(let i=0;i<dustCount;i++){const x=(Math.random()-.5)*MAP_SIZE,z=(Math.random()-.5)*MAP_SIZE,y=.3+Math.random()*9,phase=Math.random()*6.28;dustPos[i*3]=x;dustPos[i*3+1]=y;dustPos[i*3+2]=z;dustSeedSin[i]=Math.sin(phase);dustSeedCos[i]=Math.cos(phase);}
    const dustGeo=new THREE.BufferGeometry();dustGeo.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(dustPos,3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    const dust=new THREE.Points(dustGeo,new THREE.PointsMaterial({color:0xffe5b6,size:lowSpec?.055:.075,transparent:true,opacity:.34,depthWrite:false,sizeAttenuation:true}));scene.add(dust);

    let environmentAccumulator=0;
    function updateEnvironment(dt,now){
      environmentAccumulator+=dt;if(environmentAccumulator<1/30)return;dt=environmentAccumulator;environmentAccumulator=0;
      fountainWater.material.opacity=.73+Math.sin(now*2.1)*.045;fountainWater.rotation.z+=dt*.035;
      updateAnimatedBannerMatrices(now);
      // The outline renderer is disabled in this build; avoid dirty bookkeeping
      // entirely until that feature is actually enabled.
      if(ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES)exactOutlineDynamicDirty=true;
      const lanternPhase=now*4.2,lanternSin=Math.sin(lanternPhase),lanternCos=Math.cos(lanternPhase);
      for(const l of lanterns)l.m.material.emissiveIntensity=2+(lanternSin*l.cosPhase+lanternCos*l.sinPhase)*.3;
      const dustPhase=now*.38,dustSin=Math.sin(dustPhase),dustCos=Math.cos(dustPhase),a=dustGeo.attributes.position.array;
      for(let i=0;i<dustCount;i++){const j=i*3;a[j]+=(dustSin*dustSeedCos[i]+dustCos*dustSeedSin[i])*.025*dt;a[j+1]+=.095*dt;if(a[j+1]>9.5)a[j+1]=.25;}
      dustGeo.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
    }

    // Build one exact static depth mesh for the animated-player outline pass.
    // Positions are baked in world space, including InstancedMesh transforms.
    // The only map transforms that animate are kept in dedicated dynamic batches.
    const exactOutlineDynamicSources = new Set([fountainWater, ...animatedBannerBatches]);

    // Collapse opaque, immobile map pieces without changing their geometry. On
    // WEBGL_multi_draw browsers, BatchedMesh keeps per-object frustum culling while
    // removing the old per-cell submission split. Unsupported browsers retain the
    // proven R176 spatial-merge path instead of taking BatchedMesh's slower fallback.
    function mergeStaticMapRenderMeshes() {
      const excluded = new Set([sky, fountainWater, ...animatedBannerBatches, ...lanterns.map(entry => entry.m)]);
      const groups = new Map();
      const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
      const matrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
      const LEGACY_CELL_SIZE = 42;
      const useMultiDraw = typeof THREE.BatchedMesh === 'function' && renderer.extensions.has('WEBGL_multi_draw');
      const legacyFallbackGroups = useMultiDraw ? null : new Map();
      let eligibleMeshes = 0;

      const effectivelyVisible = object => {
        for (let node = object; node; node = node.parent) if (!node.visible) return false;
        return true;
      };
      const attributeSignatureCache = new WeakMap();
      const attributeSignature = geometry => {
        let signature = attributeSignatureCache.get(geometry);
        if (signature !== undefined) return signature;
        const attrs = Object.keys(geometry.attributes).sort().map(name => {
          const a = geometry.attributes[name];
          return `${name}:${a.itemSize}:${a.normalized ? 1 : 0}:${a.array.constructor.name}`;
        }).join(',');
        const morph = Object.keys(geometry.morphAttributes || {}).sort().map(name => `${name}:${geometry.morphAttributes[name].length}`).join(',');
        signature = `${geometry.index ? geometry.index.array.constructor.name : 'none'}|${attrs}|${morph}`;
        attributeSignatureCache.set(geometry, signature);
        return signature;
      };

      // R177 deliberately batches only objects that already share the exact same
      // Material instance. Door / palm construction now reuses shared materials
      // at creation time, so there is no need to guess whether two independent
      // materials are semantically equivalent. This keeps shader state exact.
      const legacyGroups=new Map();
      scene.updateMatrixWorld(true);
      scene.traverse(node => {
        // Existing InstancedMesh props are already the optimal repeated-geometry
        // path. Do not explode them into BatchedMesh instances; this hybrid keeps
        // one instanced draw for repeated props and BatchedMesh for heterogeneous
        // static geometry sharing one material.
        if (!node.isMesh || node.isSkinnedMesh || node.isInstancedMesh || excluded.has(node)) return;
        if (!effectivelyVisible(node) || node.layers.mask !== 1 || node.renderOrder !== 0) return;
        if (!node.geometry?.attributes?.position || Array.isArray(node.material) || !node.material) return;
        if (node.material.transparent || node.material.opacity < 1 || node.material.visible === false) return;
        if (node.geometry.drawRange.start !== 0 || node.geometry.drawRange.count !== Infinity) return;
        matrix.copy(node.matrixWorld);
        if (matrix.determinant() < 0) return;
        const attrSig=attributeSignature(node.geometry);
        const key=[node.material.uuid,attrSig,node.castShadow?1:0,node.receiveShadow?1:0,node.layers.mask].join('|');
        let group=groups.get(key);
        if(!group){group={material:node.material,castShadow:node.castShadow,receiveShadow:node.receiveShadow,layerMask:node.layers.mask,nodes:[],geometries:new Map(),vertexCount:0,indexCount:0};groups.set(key,group);}
        group.nodes.push(node);
        if(!group.geometries.has(node.geometry)){
          const pos=node.geometry.attributes.position;
          group.geometries.set(node.geometry,-1);
          group.vertexCount+=pos.count;
          group.indexCount+=node.geometry.index?.count || pos.count;
        }
        worldPos.setFromMatrixPosition(matrix);
        const cellX=Math.floor((worldPos.x+MAP_HALF)/LEGACY_CELL_SIZE),cellZ=Math.floor((worldPos.z+MAP_HALF)/LEGACY_CELL_SIZE);
        const legacyKey=[node.material.uuid,attrSig,node.castShadow?1:0,node.receiveShadow?1:0,node.layers.mask,cellX,cellZ].join('|');
        legacyGroups.set(legacyKey,(legacyGroups.get(legacyKey)||0)+1);
        if(legacyFallbackGroups){
          let fallbackGroup=legacyFallbackGroups.get(legacyKey);
          if(!fallbackGroup){fallbackGroup={material:node.material,castShadow:node.castShadow,receiveShadow:node.receiveShadow,layerMask:node.layers.mask,nodes:[]};legacyFallbackGroups.set(legacyKey,fallbackGroup);}
          fallbackGroup.nodes.push(node);
        }
        eligibleMeshes++;
      });

      let batches=0,batchedMeshes=0,singles=0,uniqueGeometries=0,failedGroups=0;
      if(useMultiDraw){
        for(const group of groups.values()){
          if(group.nodes.length<2){singles+=group.nodes.length;continue;}
          let batch=null;
          try{
            batch=new THREE.BatchedMesh(group.nodes.length,group.vertexCount,group.indexCount,group.material);
            batch.name='CatStrike.StaticMapBatchedMesh';
            batch.castShadow=group.castShadow;batch.receiveShadow=group.receiveShadow;batch.layers.mask=group.layerMask;
            batch.matrixAutoUpdate=false;batch.frustumCulled=true;
            batch.perObjectFrustumCulled=true;
            // Opaque map pieces do not need depth sorting. Disabling it removes a
            // camera-dependent JS sort while per-object frustum culling remains on.
            batch.sortObjects=false;
            // three r185 WebGLRenderer checks object.colorTexture although r185's
            // BatchedMesh stores _colorsTexture. Explicit null avoids needless
            // getProgram/cache-key resolution every frame until the upstream r186 fix.
            batch.colorTexture=null;
            for(const geometry of group.geometries.keys()){
              if(!geometry.boundingBox)geometry.computeBoundingBox();
              if(!geometry.boundingSphere)geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
              group.geometries.set(geometry,batch.addGeometry(geometry));
              uniqueGeometries++;
            }
            for(const node of group.nodes){
              const geometryId=group.geometries.get(node.geometry);
              const instanceId=batch.addInstance(geometryId);
              batch.setMatrixAt(instanceId,node.matrixWorld);
            }
            batch.computeBoundingBox();batch.computeBoundingSphere();
            scene.add(batch);
            for(const node of group.nodes)if(node.parent)node.parent.remove(node);
            batchedMeshes+=group.nodes.length;batches++;
          }catch(error){
            failedGroups++;
            if(batch?.parent)batch.parent.remove(batch);
            try{batch?.dispose?.();}catch(_){}
            singles+=group.nodes.length;
            safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] BatchedMesh group fallback:',error);
          }
        }
      }else{
        // Preserve R176's exact spatial merge on browsers without multi-draw.
        // This avoids a device-specific regression (notably on engines where
        // BatchedMesh would internally fall back to many individual draw calls).
        for(const group of legacyFallbackGroups.values()){
          if(group.nodes.length<2){singles+=group.nodes.length;continue;}
          const transformed=[];
          for(const node of group.nodes){
            const geometry=node.geometry.clone();geometry.applyMatrix4(node.matrixWorld);transformed.push(geometry);
          }
          let merged=null;
          try{merged=mergeGeometries(transformed,false);}catch(_){merged=null;}
          for(const geometry of transformed)geometry.dispose();
          if(!merged){failedGroups++;singles+=group.nodes.length;continue;}
          merged.computeBoundingBox();merged.computeBoundingSphere();
          const batch=new THREE.Mesh(merged,group.material);
          batch.name='CatStrike.StaticMapSpatialFallback';
          batch.castShadow=group.castShadow;batch.receiveShadow=group.receiveShadow;batch.layers.mask=group.layerMask;
          batch.matrixAutoUpdate=false;batch.frustumCulled=true;scene.add(batch);
          for(const node of group.nodes)if(node.parent)node.parent.remove(node);
          batchedMeshes+=group.nodes.length;batches++;
        }
      }

      let legacyDraws=0;
      for(const count of legacyGroups.values())legacyDraws+=count>=2?1:count;
      const optimizedDraws=batches+singles;
      const structuralReduction=optimizedDraws?legacyDraws/optimizedDraws:1;

      for (const object of mapObjects) {
        if (object?.isGroup && object.children.length === 0 && object.parent) object.parent.remove(object);
      }
      const emptyGroups = [];
      scene.traverse(node => {
        if (node.isGroup && node.children.length === 0 && node.parent && node !== objectiveVisuals && node !== bombVisual) emptyGroups.push(node);
      });
      for (const group of emptyGroups) if (group.parent) group.parent.remove(group);
      mapObjects.length = 0;

      window.CatStrikeMapRenderStats={path:useMultiDraw?'multi-draw':'spatial-fallback',eligibleMeshes,batchedMeshes,batches,singles,uniqueGeometries,failedGroups,legacyDraws,optimizedDraws,structuralReduction};
      safeConsoleInfo('[CatStrike] Static map renderer:',useMultiDraw?'multi-draw':'spatial-fallback',eligibleMeshes,'eligible ->',batches,'batches +',singles,'singles; R176 spatial draws',legacyDraws,'->',optimizedDraws,`(${structuralReduction.toFixed(2)}x fewer)`,'failed groups',failedGroups);
      return window.CatStrikeMapRenderStats;
    }

    try { mergeStaticMapRenderMeshes(); }
    catch (error) { safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] Static map render batching fallback:', error); }

    function freezeStaticMapTransforms(){
      const moving = new Set([camera, fountainWater, bombVisual, ...animatedBannerBatches]);
      scene.updateMatrixWorld(true);
      let frozen=0;
      const freezeNode=(node,dynamicAncestor=false)=>{
        const dynamic=dynamicAncestor||moving.has(node);
        if(!dynamic){
          node.updateMatrix();node.matrixAutoUpdate=false;
          if('matrixWorldAutoUpdate' in node)node.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;
          frozen++;
        }
        for(const child of node.children)freezeNode(child,dynamic);
      };
      for(const child of scene.children)freezeNode(child,false);
      safeConsoleInfo('[CatStrike] Frozen static scene transforms:',frozen);
    }
    try { freezeStaticMapTransforms(); }
    catch(error){ safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] Static transform freeze fallback:',error); }

    function buildExactOutlineOccluderMesh() {
      const cells = new Map();
      const instanceMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
      const worldMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
      const transformedMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
      const worldPosition = new THREE.Vector3();
      const boundsCenter = new THREE.Vector3();
      const CELL_SIZE = 32;
      let totalRecords = 0;
      let totalVertices = 0;

      const effectivelyVisible = object => {
        for (let node = object; node; node = node.parent) if (!node.visible) return false;
        return true;
      };
      const addRecord = (node, matrix) => {
        const geometry = node.geometry;
        const position = geometry?.attributes?.position;
        if (!position || position.count < 3) return;
        worldPosition.setFromMatrixPosition(matrix);
        // Skyline and mountain silhouettes sit beyond the collision perimeter and
        // can never lie between two playable entities, so they add no useful depth.
        if (Math.abs(worldPosition.x) > MAP_HALF + 5 || Math.abs(worldPosition.z) > MAP_HALF + 5) return;
        if (!geometry.boundingSphere) geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
        if(geometry.boundingSphere)boundsCenter.copy(geometry.boundingSphere.center).applyMatrix4(matrix);
        else boundsCenter.setFromMatrixPosition(matrix);
        const key = `${Math.floor(boundsCenter.x / CELL_SIZE)},${Math.floor(boundsCenter.z / CELL_SIZE)}`;
        let cell = cells.get(key);
        if (!cell) {
          cell = { records: [], vertexCount: 0, indexCount: 0 };
          cells.set(key, cell);
        }
        const indexCount = geometry.index?.count || position.count;
        cell.records.push({ geometry, matrix: matrix.clone() });
        cell.vertexCount += position.count;
        cell.indexCount += indexCount;
        totalRecords++;
        totalVertices += position.count;
      };

      scene.updateMatrixWorld(true);
      scene.traverse(node => {
        if (!node.isMesh || node.isSkinnedMesh || node === sky || node.userData?.skipExactOutlineOccluder || exactOutlineDynamicSources.has(node) || !effectivelyVisible(node)) return;
        if (node.isInstancedMesh) {
          for (let i = 0; i < node.count; i++) {
            node.getMatrixAt(i, instanceMatrix);
            transformedMatrix.multiplyMatrices(node.matrixWorld, instanceMatrix);
            addRecord(node, transformedMatrix);
          }
        } else {
          worldMatrix.copy(node.matrixWorld);
          addRecord(node, worldMatrix);
        }
      });

      if (!cells.size || !totalVertices) return null;
      const group = new THREE.Group();
      group.name = 'CatStrike.StaticExactOutlineOccluderCells';
      group.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
      const point = new THREE.Vector3();
      let builtCells = 0;

      for (const [key, cell] of cells) {
        if (!cell.vertexCount || !cell.indexCount) continue;
        const positions = new Float32Array(cell.vertexCount * 3);
        const IndexArray = cell.vertexCount > 65535 ? Uint32Array : Uint16Array;
        const indices = new IndexArray(cell.indexCount);
        let vertexOffset = 0;
        let positionOffset = 0;
        let indexOffset = 0;

        for (const record of cell.records) {
          const geometry = record.geometry;
          const sourcePosition = geometry.attributes.position;
          for (let i = 0; i < sourcePosition.count; i++) {
            point.fromBufferAttribute(sourcePosition, i).applyMatrix4(record.matrix);
            positions[positionOffset++] = point.x;
            positions[positionOffset++] = point.y;
            positions[positionOffset++] = point.z;
          }
          if (geometry.index) {
            const sourceIndex = geometry.index;
            for (let i = 0; i < sourceIndex.count; i++) indices[indexOffset++] = vertexOffset + sourceIndex.getX(i);
          } else {
            for (let i = 0; i < sourcePosition.count; i++) indices[indexOffset++] = vertexOffset + i;
          }
          vertexOffset += sourcePosition.count;
        }

        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
        geometry.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(indices, 1));
        geometry.computeBoundingBox();
        geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
        const occluder = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, modelOutlineDepthMaterial);
        occluder.name = `CatStrike.StaticExactOutlineOccluders.${key}`;
        occluder.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
        occluder.frustumCulled = true;
        group.add(occluder);
        builtCells++;
      }

      if (!builtCells) return null;
      group.updateMatrixWorld(true);
      group.traverse(node=>{if('matrixWorldAutoUpdate' in node)node.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;});
      exactOutlineDynamicOccluderScene.add(group);
      safeConsoleInfo('[CatStrike] Exact outline occluders spatially merged:', totalRecords, 'parts,', totalVertices, 'vertices in', builtCells, 'cells.');
      return group;
    }
    function buildExactOutlineDynamicOccluders() {
      if (animatedBanners.length) {
        exactOutlineBannerOccluder = new THREE.InstancedMesh(
          bannerFlagGeometry,
          modelOutlineDepthMaterial,
          animatedBanners.length
        );
        exactOutlineBannerOccluder.name = 'CatStrike.DynamicBannerOutlineOccluders';
        exactOutlineBannerOccluder.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
        exactOutlineBannerOccluder.frustumCulled = false;
        exactOutlineDynamicOccluderScene.add(exactOutlineBannerOccluder);
      }
      exactOutlineFountainOccluder = new THREE.Mesh(fountainWater.geometry, modelOutlineDepthMaterial);
      exactOutlineFountainOccluder.name = 'CatStrike.DynamicFountainOutlineOccluder';
      exactOutlineFountainOccluder.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
      exactOutlineFountainOccluder.frustumCulled = false;
      exactOutlineDynamicOccluderScene.add(exactOutlineFountainOccluder);
      syncExactOutlineDynamicOccluders();
    }

    const exactOutlineBannerMatrix=new THREE.Matrix4();
    function syncExactOutlineDynamicOccluders() {
      if (!exactOutlineDynamicDirty) return;
      const matrix=exactOutlineBannerMatrix;
      if (exactOutlineBannerOccluder) {
        for (let i = 0; i < animatedBanners.length; i++) {
          const source=animatedBanners[i];
          source.batch.getMatrixAt(source.batchIndex,matrix);
          exactOutlineBannerOccluder.setMatrixAt(i,matrix);
        }
        exactOutlineBannerOccluder.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
      }
      if (exactOutlineFountainOccluder) {
        fountainWater.updateWorldMatrix(true, false);
        exactOutlineFountainOccluder.matrix.copy(fountainWater.matrixWorld);
        exactOutlineFountainOccluder.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate = true;
      }
      exactOutlineDynamicDirty = false;
    }

    if (ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES) {
      try {
        exactOutlineOccluderMesh = buildExactOutlineOccluderMesh();
        if (exactOutlineOccluderMesh) buildExactOutlineDynamicOccluders();
      }
      catch (error) {
        exactOutlineOccluderMesh = null;
        safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] Static outline occluder merge fallback:', error);
      }
    }

    loadingUI?.set(74, 'BUIWDING THE CITY...', 'Map collision, props, lighting, and bombsites are ready.');
    await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));

    // ---------- Embedded Boykisser model ----------
    // Embedded CC-BY-4.0 Boykisser mesh. Source and attribution are in the legal footer.
    const BOYKISSER_FULL = {
      vertexCount: 14014, indexCount: 77628, index32: false,
      positions:
